### [1] [Concurrent Entanglement Purification Scheduling in Dynamic Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26900)
*Gongyu Ni,Lester Ho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究时隙化、异构链路量子网络中并发纠缠纯化调度问题，提出以最大化调度请求数并兼顾max-min公平性的MILP调度器，以及基于DQN/PPO/SAC的强化学习调度器，仿真表明RL成功率最高（较MILP提升最多6%，较Progressive Filling与Greedy分别提升16.5%和19.5%），但Bell对消耗更高且少量请求被推迟；MILP在多数场景公平性最高、Bell对消耗最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络需提供满足应用保真度要求的端到端纠缠，多跳路径通常需沿路执行纠缠纯化。现有调度方法或仅在链路级纯化，或针对静态请求集优化端到端保真度，无法刻画随时间竞争网络资源的并发请求。

Method: 建模时隙化量子网络，链路异构，请求竞争链路容量与量子存储器，且每个请求可由多条并行路径服务以提高可靠性。构建混合整数线性规划（MILP）调度器，以最大化调度请求数为目标，并以max-min路径分配作为公平性准则；同时设计基于DQN、PPO与SAC的强化学习调度器，其奖励在请求成功与Bell对消耗之间进行权衡。在不同网络负载、拓扑与目标保真度阈值下进行仿真比较。

Result: RL调度器取得最高成功率，较MILP最多提升6%，较Progressive Filling和Greedy启发式分别提升16.5%与19.5%，代价是更高的Bell对消耗及少量请求被推迟；MILP在多数场景下公平性最高且Bell对消耗最低。

Conclusion: 面向并发请求的端到端纠缠纯化调度中，RL方法在成功率上占优，而MILP在公平性与资源效率（Bell对消耗）上更优，二者存在明确的性能—成本权衡，可按应用侧重选择。

Abstract: Quantum networks must deliver end-to-end entanglement whose fidelity meets application requirements, which typically requires entanglement purification along multi-hop paths. Existing scheduling approaches either purify at the link level or optimize end-to-end fidelity for a static set of requests, and do not capture concurrent requests competing for network resources over time. This paper studies concurrent entanglement purification scheduling in a time-slotted quantum network with heterogeneous links, in which requests compete for link capacity and quantum memories, and each request may be served by multiple parallel paths for increased reliability. We formulate a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) scheduler that maximizes the number of scheduled requests, with max-min path allocation as a fairness criterion, and develop reinforcement learning (RL) schedulers based on DQN, PPO, and SAC, whose rewards balance request success against Bell-pair consumption. Simulations under varying network load, topologies and target fidelity thresholds show that the RL schedulers achieve the highest success rates, improving on MILP by up to 6% and on Progressive Filling and Greedy heuristics by 16.5% and 19.5% respectively, at the cost of higher Bell pair consumption and the deferral of a small fraction of requests. MILP, in contrast, achieves the highest fairness and the lowest Bell pair consumption in most scenarios.

</details>


### [2] [Efficient Ground-State Manifold Amplification for Rydberg Atom Arrays via Houdayer Cluster Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28528)
*Andrew J. Ochoa,Martin J. A. Schuetz,Helmut G. Katzgraber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种混合量子—经典方法：量子硬件提供最大独立集问题的初始解，Houdayer 簇更新算法对量子采样结果进行经典后处理，可将解多样性提升最高约11923倍（中位数24.3倍），并回收中位21%的基态流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优化设备虽能生成高质量解，但存在内在采样局限，难以产生大量互不相关的最优解；而对需要完整或大量等价最优解的许多应用（如基态流形/简并度刻画），传统量子采样能力不足。

Method: 采用混合量子—经典流程：由 QuEra Aquila 模拟退火/模拟量子器件产生最大独立集问题的初始解，再以经典 Houdayer 簇更新算法对解集进行确定性后处理扩增；在43个具有已知精确基态简并度的问题实例上评估，覆盖不同问题规模与硬度参数。

Result: 多样性放大因子最高达11923倍，跨实例中位数24.3倍；后处理平均回收中位21%的基态流形，并在6个实例上可证明完整覆盖全部流形；部分情形下经典后处理还能找到比量子硬件更大的独立集，即同时改善解质量。

Conclusion: 该方法非遍历、无法保证完整流形覆盖，但能稳定获得更多高质量、甚至严格更优的构型，说明简单经典后处理即可显著提升量子优化结果的实用价值。

Abstract: We demonstrate a hybrid quantum-classical approach for exploring ground-state manifolds of maximum independent set problems, in which quantum hardware supplies the initial solutions and an entirely classical post-processing algorithm expands them, increasing the solution diversity of quantum hardware measurements by up to several orders of magnitude. Quantum optimization devices, while capable of generating high-quality solutions, can suffer from fundamental sampling limitations. This is particularly problematic for applications that strongly depend on a large variety of uncorrelated optimal solutions. We apply the Houdayer cluster update algorithm to systematically expand quantum-generated solution sets through classical post-processing. We validate our approach on a test bed of 43 problem instances from QuEra's Aquila analog annealing device, achieving diversity amplification factors, defined as the increase in the number of distinct solutions at a given set size, of up to 11923x with a median of 24.3x across different problem scales and hardness parameters. Because exact ground-state degeneracies are known for these instances, the recovered fraction of the manifold can be measured rather than bounded: post-processing recovers a median of 21% of the ground-state manifold, and provably all of it for six instances. We also demonstrate that, in some cases, the classical post-processing can discover independent sets larger than those found by the quantum hardware, thus improving on solution quality and not just diversity. While the method is non-ergodic and cannot guarantee complete manifold coverage, it consistently discovers a larger number of high-quality, and in some cases strictly better, configurations than quantum sampling alone. This work demonstrates how simple classical post-processing can enhance the utility of quantum optimization results.

</details>


### [3] [Polynomial-Time Classical Simulation of BosonSampling under Any Constant-Fraction Photon Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27065)
*Byeongseon Go,Hyunseok Jeong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出光子损失玻色采样的多项式时间经典模拟算法：只要存活光子比例为常数，即可在Haar随机干涉仪下以高概率高效采样，显著扩大了已知可经典模拟的光子损失区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子损失如何改变玻色采样的经典复杂度，是评估近期光子量子优势鲁棒性的关键问题。此前仅知存活光子数 n=O(√(εN)) 或透过率 η=O(√(ε/N)) 时可经典模拟，线性存活这一实验相关区间是否仍难以经典模拟尚不清楚。

Method: 针对标准固定损失模型（N 个输入光子在 M 模干涉仪中传播，恰好随机均匀损失 N-n 个，输出 n 个存活光子），构造多项式时间经典采样算法；再将该固定损失分析推广至均匀透射损失模型（透过率 η）。

Result: 对任意常数存活比例 n/N∈(0,1)，算法在 poly(M,N,ε⁻¹,δ⁻¹) 时间内，以至少 1-δ 的概率（对 Haar 随机干涉仪）输出与固定损失玻色采样分布总变差距离 ε 内的样本；均匀透射损失模型下对任意固定 η∈(0,1) 亦得到类似多项式时间可模拟性。

Conclusion: 结果大幅扩展了已知可证可经典模拟的区域（从 n=O(√(εN)) 及 η=O(√(ε/N)) 到任意常数存活比例与固定透过率），表明在本工作所考虑的条件下，即使实验相关的线性存活区间也可被高效经典模拟。

Abstract: Understanding how photon loss changes the classical complexity of BosonSampling is central to assessing the robustness of near-term photonic quantum advantage. In this work, we present a polynomial-time classical simulation algorithm for lossy BosonSampling that applies whenever a constant fraction of the input photons survives. We consider the standard fixed-loss model, where $N$ input photons propagate through an $M$-mode interferometer and exactly $N-n$ photons are lost uniformly at random, leaving $n$ surviving photons at the output. For any constant surviving fraction $n/N\in(0,1)$, the algorithm samples from a distribution within total variation distance $\varepsilon$ of the fixed-loss BosonSampling distribution with probability at least $1-δ$ over Haar-random interferometers, in time $\operatorname{poly}(M,N,\varepsilon^{-1},δ^{-1})$. We further extend the fixed-loss analysis to the uniform-transmission-loss model and obtain analogous polynomial-time simulability for any fixed transmission rate $η\in(0,1)$. These results substantially extend the known provably simulable regime of $n=O(\sqrt{\varepsilon N})$ surviving photons or $η=O(\sqrt{\varepsilon/N})$ transmission rate, and demonstrate that even the experimentally relevant linear-survival regime can admit efficient classical simulation under the conditions considered here.

</details>


### [4] [Cavity models of non-Abelian gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27070)
*Lucia Valor,Jacquelin Luneau,Klaus Liegener,Stefan Filipp,Peter Rabl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 作者类比腔QED，从Yang-Mills拉氏量出发在库仑规范下导出SU(2)腔哈密顿量（仅三个局域玻色模），得到含束缚"胶球"激发谱，并推广到一维耦合SU(2)腔阵列，讨论胶球凝聚的驱动激发与多胶球团簇，提出用超导量子电路实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔量子电动力学能用少量原子能级与孤立光子模刻画束缚电荷与电磁场的低能性质，但非阿贝尔规范理论尚缺乏类似的"腔化"低能有效框架；作者希望建立SU(2)纯规范动力学的类似构造，以在粒子物理与量子光学的交叉界面研究新奇多体物理。

Method: 以SU(2) Yang-Mills拉氏量为起点，在库仑规范下做模式截断/局域化，导出仅含三个局域玻色模的SU(2)腔哈密顿量；分析其激发谱并与非相互作用电磁情形对比；再将模型推广为一维耦合SU(2)腔阵列，研究驱动激发与多胶球态聚集两类相互作用诱导过程；并设计与超导量子电路的实验映射。

Result: 所得SU(2)腔模型呈现非平凡激发谱：最低能组态为复合规范粒子（"胶球"）而非单模激发；在一维阵列中识别出胶球凝聚的驱动激发和多胶球团簇两类基本相互作用诱导过程，并给出其在现有超导量子电路上的可实验实现方案。

Conclusion: 腔QED式的低能有效描述可推广到非阿贝尔规范理论，SU(2)腔框架自然产生胶球束缚态及丰富的多体现象，并可由当前超导量子电路实验探索，为粒子物理与量子光学交叉研究提供新平台。

Abstract: In quantum optics and related fields, cavity quantum electrodynamics provides a powerful framework for modeling the low-energy properties of bound charges coupled to electromagnetic fields, using a minimal set of atomic levels and isolated photonic modes. Here, we develop a similar framework for non-Abelian gauge theories, focusing on the pure-gauge dynamics of SU(2) fields as the conceptually simplest example. Starting from the corresponding Yang-Mills Lagrangian, we derive an SU(2) cavity Hamiltonian in the Coulomb gauge, represented in terms of only three localized bosonic modes. Unlike its non-interacting electromagnetic counterpart, this model exhibits a nontrivial excitation spectrum featuring bound composite gauge particles ('glueballs') as the lowest-energy configurations. We extend this framework to one-dimensional arrays of coupled SU(2) cavities and discuss the driven excitation of a glueball condensate and the clustering of multi-glueball states as two elementary interaction-induced processes in these models. These examples offer a glimpse into the multitude of novel many-body phenomena that arise within this cavity SU(2) framework and we show how this physics at the interface between particle physics and quantum optics can be experimentally explored using state-of-the-art superconducting quantum circuits.

</details>


### [5] [Rényi multi-entropy of graph states and the $n -> 1$ limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27080)
*Sriram Akella,Abhijit Gadde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子比特图态上的Rényi多熵：证明奇数n时复制收缩等于2^{-k/2}（k为有限域上矩阵秩之和），对"碰撞受控"图给出关于n的多项式并作n→1连续化，且发现该连续化在Pauli测量下非单调，故不是纠缠单调量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi多熵将Rényi熵推广到多体纯态，全息上猜想其n→1极限对应体区极小皂膜网面积；但一般态下复制收缩不是密度矩阵的矩，n=1连续化困难，需寻找可计算的具体态类来理解其性质。

Method: 针对量子比特图态：构造整数n处每量子比特一行一列的有限域矩阵，证明奇数n时复制收缩等于2^{-k/2}，k为这些矩阵秩之和；定义"碰撞受控"图类（含无偶环图与所有完全二部图），其秩和是n的多项式；采用自然的n→1多项式连续化，表示为各party划分上的有限求和，并检验其在单量子比特Pauli测量下的单调性。

Result: 每个奇数n下收缩均为2^{-k/2}；碰撞受控图上秩和为n的多项式；Rényi多熵在每个奇数n>1时对单量子比特Pauli测量单调不增，但n→1连续化不单调：对2r+1≥7的奇数环测量一个量子比特的Z，使其从(r+1)log2升至(2r-1)log2。

Conclusion: 图态上多熵的复制收缩可由有限域矩阵秩精确刻画，n→1连续化虽在整数点具单调性，却破坏纠缠单调性要求，说明该连续化不是纠缠单调量，多熵的一般态定义与全息对应仍需更审慎的连续化方案。

Abstract: Rényi multi-entropy extends the Rényi entropy to pure states of more than two parties. It is defined by a replica contraction at integer replica number $n$, and the multi-entropy is its $n\to1$ limit. In holography this limit is conjectured to compute the area of a minimal soap-film web in the bulk. Understanding multi-entropy for general states, however, is harder. Unlike the bipartite case, the replica contraction is not a moment of a density matrix, and the continuation to $n=1$ has to be found by other means. We study this problem for qubit graph states. We show that at every odd $n$ the contraction equals $2^{-k/2}$, where $k$ is a sum of ranks of matrices over a finite field, one row and one column per qubit. We define a class of graphs that we call collision-controlled, for which the rank sum is a polynomial in $n$. This class includes graphs without even cycles and all complete bipartite graphs. The natural polynomial $n \to 1$ continuation is a finite sum over partitions of the parties. While the Rényi multi-entropy is non-increasing under single-qubit Pauli measurements at every odd integer $n>1$, the continuation to $n=1$ is not. A $Z$ measurement on one qubit of an odd ring with $2r + 1 \geq 7$ qubits raises it from $(r+1)\log 2$ to $(2r-1) \log 2$. Thus the continuation is not an entanglement monotone.

</details>


### [6] [Active isolation of transmon readout with a tunable inductive bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27137)
*Chunyang Ding,Chuyao Tong,Paul Varosy,Nikitha Jain,Ziyi Zhao,Eric I. Rosenthal,Konrad W. Lehnert,David I. Schuster*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用芯片级可调电感桥（微波开关）替代读出链中的铁氧体隔离器，验证其在固定频率transmon读出中对量子比特相干性无显著影响，并可抑制放大器热噪声引起的退相干30 dB以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子计算规模化需要快速高保真读出，而低温放大器的热噪声会使量子比特退相干，当前必须依赖额外的铁氧体隔离器抑制噪声，这与芯片集成化目标相冲突。

Method: 将芯片级微波开关（可调电感桥）集成到固定频率transmon的读出链中，通过磁通脉冲驱动开关仅在读出窗口导通，并测量其对量子比特相干性、残余激发态布居与读出保真度的影响；同时注入模拟放大器热光子的宽带噪声，测试开关对退相干的抑制能力。

Result: 脉冲式开关工作对量子比特相干性、残余激发态布居和读出保真度指标无明显影响；在宽带噪声注入下，开关将噪声导致的退相干抑制超过30 dB，与独立测量的开关通断比一致。

Conclusion: 芯片级微波开关可作为读出链隔离器的潜在片上集成替代方案，为可扩展超导量子计算读出架构提供新路径。

Abstract: Scalable superconducting quantum computation requires fast, high-fidelity readout. Currently, this is assisted through cryogenic amplification of the readout signal. In traditional dispersive readout schemes, the amplifiers that are used to provide high signal-to-noise ratio also generate thermal noise that can decohere the qubits, demanding additional ferrite-based isolators to suppress this noise. Here, we integrate a chip-scale microwave switch, the tunable inductive bridge, into the readout chain of a fixed-frequency transmon. Flux-pulsing the switch, such that it transmits only during the readout, did not make a discernable difference to qubit coherence, residual excited-state population, or readout fidelity metrics. By injecting broadband noise that mimics amplifier thermal photons, we show that the switch suppresses the resulting qubit dephasing by more than $30$~dB, consistent with its independently measured on-off ratio, establishing it as a potential chip-based integrated replacement for readout-chain isolators.

</details>


### [7] [The next phase of quantum energy science: from mechanisms to applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27165)
*Florian Metzler,Matt K. Lilley,Matthew C. DeCapua,Peter L. Hagelstein,Jonah F. Messinger,Nicola Galvanetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以"量子能量科学"统合量子电池、分子/激子能量输运、极化激元学、量子材料与核控制等分散领域，用"机制—功能—能力—应用"框架梳理，归纳五种基本功能（激发、保持、释放、传输、转化）并讨论其在不同尺度上的实现难度差异，进而提出存储、路由、转化三项工程能力及相应性能指标与远景目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个原本互不相关的研究群体（量子电池、分子与激子能量输运、极化激元学、量子材料、核控制）都开始以"量子能量"为名描述工作，但缺乏统一的概念框架；同时该领域与量子信息科学存在关键区别——无需始终完整保留量子态，工程化耗散或退相干甚至可能改善目标结果，因此需要一套专门的组织框架来界定领域、对齐术语并指引技术转化。

Method: 采用概念性/综述性框架构建方法：以"有意识地利用量子态、耦合、集体效应与工程化环境来调控能量状态与通路"作为领域共同内核，建立连接机制、功能、能力与应用的四层框架；提炼五种基本功能（Excite、Hold、Release、Transfer、Transform）并考察其背后的物理机制；跨分子尺度到核尺度检验功能的普适性；再从技术走向出发，归纳三项工程能力（Storage、Routing、Conversion），并以对应用至关重要的性能指标加以刻画，最后用"拉伸情景"（stretch scenarios）设定可使能力具备技术意义的性能目标。

Result: 提出一个统一的四层组织框架（机制—功能—能力—应用）；识别出五种在分子至核尺度反复出现的基本功能，并指出其相对实现难度可能差异显著；界定存储、路由、转化三项工程能力及关键性能指标；通过拉伸情景给出使这些能力在技术上具有实质意义的性能目标。

Conclusion: "量子能量科学"可作为统一新兴交叉领域的有效框架，其核心在于对能量状态与通路的量子调控而非量子态的完整保存；五功能—三能力的结构既适用于跨尺度比较，也为技术评估提供了指标化路径，拉伸情景则为该领域指明了可检验的性能里程碑。

Abstract: A growing research community spanning quantum batteries, molecular and excitonic energy transport, polaritonics, quantum materials and nuclear control has begun to describe its work in terms of quantum energy or quantum energy science. What connects these otherwise disparate efforts is the deliberate use of quantum states, couplings, collective effects and engineered environments to control energetic states and pathways. A key difference from quantum information science is that the complete quantum state does not always need to be preserved; engineered dissipation or dephasing can even improve the desired outcome. We organize this emerging domain through a framework that connects mechanisms, functions, capabilities, and applications. We identify five elementary functions (Excite, Hold, Release, Transfer, and Transform) and examine the mechanisms that implement them. The same functions recur from the molecular to the nuclear scale, although their relative implementation difficulty can differ sharply. Moving toward technology, we highlight three engineering capabilities (Storage, Routing, and Conversion) through the performance metrics that matter for their applications. Stretch scenarios illustrate what performance targets could make these capabilities technologically consequential.

</details>


### [8] [Quantum Fluctuations and Wigner Dynamics of a Carbon Nanotube Oscillator Driven by an Atomic Force Microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27167)
*Conrad Silio,Cristian Staii*

In the classical-noise limit, the three independent entries of the finite-time covariance matrix reduce to explicit finite sums over these response poles, eliminating numerical double-time covariance integration. We extend the construction to the full quantum fluctuation spectrum, obtaining a thermal frequency series representation that retains zero-point and low-temperature fluctuations. For an initial two-packet superposition, we examine interference attenuation and the integrated negative part of the Wigner function, which provides a phase space measure of nonclassicality. The same moments further yield a diagnostic chain for the window conditions of local rotational invariance -- an exact drift-kernel identity, a proven single-state identifiability boundary, and a transferability census over couplings and realizations -- a finite-order framework, not a proof of universal emergence.

Conclusion: 统计效率与实现成本之间存在本质权衡：减少重复次数需要更昂贵的电路，且该权衡在编码后依然成立；不同容错范式与码的选择给出不同资源画像，作者由此提供了跨计算范式的显式资源估计。

Abstract: The three-tangle, $τ_3$, is a measure of genuine tripartite entanglement that distinguishes GHZ-type from W-type entanglement in pure three-qubit states. These results reveal a tradeoff between statistical efficiency and implementation cost, since reducing the number of repetitions requires increasingly expensive circuits. We then show that this tradeoff persists once the circuits are encoded. In a fully fault-tolerant setting, the increased click probability comes with a substantial non-Clifford synthesis cost, while an early fault-tolerant implementation based on $T$ gates and STAR rotations reduces this overhead at the expense of additional encoded resources. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般认为干净、相互作用、周期驱动的系统会热化到无特征的无限温态，难以形成稳定有序态；已有的prethermal序并非真正的驱动稳态序，作者希望在平均能量算符的基态层面找到与之本质不同的稳定量子序。

Method: 将时间演化分解为几何部分与平均能量动力学，用几何Floquet理论定义Floquet基态；推导通用的变分原理以在大规模相互作用驱动系统中计算Floquet基态，并据此构建矩阵乘积态（MPS）算法，对超过100个相互作用自旋的非可积链进行数值模拟。

Result: 模拟显示在扩展的参数区间存在有能隙、满足面积律纠缠的Floquet基态；其基态流形既可支持修饰的平衡序（如Floquet反铁磁），又可支持本质非平衡序（如基态时间晶体）；该序由有限平均能隙保护，随参数改变在相变点关闭。进一步将Floquet物相分解为平衡与非平衡要素：任一瞬间的Floquet基态是某一平衡物相的表示，非平衡内容完全来自驱动的几何holonomy。

Conclusion: 识别出准局域counterdiabatic驱动作为非可积量子系统中能隙Floquet基态抗加热稳定性的机制，并论证Floquet基态规避本征态热化假说（ETH）；该工作为深入研究多体非平衡物相铺路，并为强关联有序量子态的工程化奠定基础。

Abstract: Clean, interacting, periodically driven systems are believed to thermalize to featureless, infinite-temperature states that preclude the formation of stable ordered states. Here, we show stable quantum order, distinct from prethermal order, and its phase transitions in the ground state of the average-energy operator. Decomposing the time evolution into geometric and average-energy dynamics, we apply Geometric Floquet theory to identify the Floquet ground state. We derive a general variational principle to compute the Floquet ground state in large interacting periodically driven systems, and use it to develop a matrix product state algorithm. In driven nonintegrable chains of more than 100 interacting spins, our numerical simulations exhibit a gapped, area-law entangled Floquet ground state in extended parameter regimes; the ground-state manifold can support both dressed equilibrium order (e.g., Floquet antiferromagnets) and inherently nonequilibrium order (e.g., ground-state time crystals). This quantum order is protected by a finite average-energy gap, which closes at phase transition points upon changing model parameters. We decompose Floquet phases of matter into their equilibrium and nonequilibrium elements: whereas the Floquet ground state at any fixed time is a representative of an equilibrium phase of matter, the nonequilibrium content is entirely produced by the drive holonomy and is geometric. Finally, we identify quasilocal counterdiabatic driving as a mechanism for the stability against heating of gapped Floquet ground states in nonintegrable quantum systems, and argue why Floquet ground states evade the Eigenstate Thermalization Hypothesis. Our work enables the study of interacting nonequilibrium matter deep into the many-body regime, and lays the foundations for the engineering of strongly correlated ordered quantum states.

Abstract: Debugging quantum programs remains challenging due to the nature of quantum measurement. As quantum algorithms and systems scale, it is increasingly crucial to develop rigorous debugging and verification approaches. Statistical assertion-based debugging validates quantum program behavior through repeated circuit execution and statistical hypothesis testing, but existing frameworks treat shot count as a fixed input parameter, leaving the problem of optimal shot allocation across multiple mid-circuit assertions unexplored. To improve debugging efficiency by making better use of given shot counts, this paper proposes and evaluates four shot allocation strategies within the QDB quantum debugger implemented within the CUDA-Q framework. These allocation approaches: equal split, static weight allocation, adaptive entropy routing, and sequential retirement, use statistical approaches to test whether qubits have expected state characteristics at different points in execution. We evaluate these allocation strategies on different circuits, under both noise-free and depolarizing noise conditions. We show how adaptive strategies use shots efficiently by concentrating them on assertions with high uncertainty. This substantially reduces total shot usage while maintaining comparable debugging confidence.

Abstract: Quantum batteries can outperform their classical counterparts through collective quantum effects, yet the conditions enabling such advantage remain unclear. Using a generic class of free-fermionic chargers we prove that global operators are insufficient for scalable superextensive charging and that a cumulative hierarchy of many-body operators is required, with the structure of the battery determining the degree of advantage. This is obtained by relating the charging power to the norm of the commutator between the battery and charger Hamiltonians and deriving an upper bound only capturing noncommuting terms. Moreover, we revisit the concept of participation number and propose an effective definition that accounts for the relative strength of the contributing terms, correlating it with the charging power scaling. Not limited to quadratic Hamiltonians, our results establish cumulative locality as a key resource for quantum charging advantage.

Abstract: Given an open quantum system $S$ with environment $E$, we establish a one-to-one correspondence between completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) maps from $S$ to $E$ and assignment maps initializing the composite system $SE$ in a possibly correlated state prior to unitary evolution. We show that every possible joint state $ρ_{SE}$ can be realized by such assignment maps, which are inherently Hermiticity-preserving and trace-preserving (HPTP). While these maps sacrifice global positivity, we derive a simple necessary and sufficient condition for any local state of $S$ to belong to their domain of validity. Furthermore, we prove that the geometry of this domain is governed by the joint state assigned to the maximally mixed input, ensuring that a non-zero volume of admissible local states can readily coexist with initial system-environment entanglement. Finally, we provide an operational protocol for witnessing such limits of validity.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Choi-Jamiołkowski同构以最大纠缠态向量为"种子"建立从完全正映射空间CP(H,K)到密度算子空间（K⊗H上）的映射，并推广为酉映射。作者认为这种依赖纠缠态种子的构造方式在概念上并非必要，因此希望用范畴论中的自然性概念来揭示该构造的本质、相关公式的形式，并探讨是否存在无需纠缠向量的版本。

Method: 以Eilenberg-MacLane自然性为工具，考察在有限维Hilbert空间H、K上对两者均自然的对应关系。作者区分了值域取在L(H,K)（配Hilbert-Schmidt内积）、K⊗H*以及K⊗H上的情形，系统刻画双自然(binatural)映射与仅对K自然的映射，并引入规范关联的酉映射H→H*来分析后者。

Result: 存在唯一的对H和K均自然的"Choi映射"对应，其输出为L(H,K)或K⊗H*上的密度算子，而非K⊗H；因此无需纠缠态输入。不存在取值于K⊗H的双自然映射；仅对K自然的映射与酉映射H→H*规范关联，且可由双自然映射导出。该构造可恢复标准CJ公式。

Conclusion: Choi-Jamiołkowski同构本质上是自然性现象，纠缠态种子并非必要条件；标准CJ公式正是自然性的表达。以K⊗H为载体的版本缺乏自然性，而K⊗H*（或L(H,K)）上的版本才是自然的。

Abstract: Provided a maximally entangled state vector in ${\mathcal{H}}\otimes{\mathcal{H}}$, the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism of quantum information theory (or channel/state duality) produces a mapping of ${\mathsf CP}({\mathcal{H}},{\mathcal{K}})$ (completely positive maps of density operators on ${\mathcal{H}}$ tothose on ${\mathcal{K}}$) onto the density operators on ${\mathcal{K}}\otimes{\mathcal{H}}$, which extends to a unitary map. Here, ${\mathcal{H}}$ and ${\mathcal{K}}$ are {finite-dimensional} Hilbert spaces.The nature of this construction, the form of the associated formulas, and the possibility of versions which do not require an entangled vector seed, are illuminated from the perspective of {naturality}, in the sense of Eilenberg and MacLane.There is a unique such correspondence (``Choi map'') natural in both ${\mathcal{H}}$ and ${\mathcal{K}}$ which produces density operators on either ${\mathsf L}({\mathcal{H}},{\mathcal{K}})$ (Hilbert-Schmidt inner product) or ${\mathcal{K}}\otimes{\mathcal{H}}^*$ instead of ${\mathcal{K}}\otimes{\mathcal{H}}$. In particular, no entangled state input is required. There are no such binatural maps carried on ${\mathcal{K}}\otimes{\mathcal{H}}$, but those natural in only ${\mathcal{K}}$ are canonically associated with unitary maps ${\mathcal{H}}\rightarrow{\mathcal{H}}^*$ and derive from the binatural maps. The construction recovers the standard Choi-Jamiolkowski formulae and shows how they express naturality.

</details>

Conclusion: Carleman线性化结合量子ODE求解可有效处理高维非线性SDE，在弱非线性弱噪声 regime 下兼具指数收敛截断误差与关于维度的多对数级复杂度，为量子求解随机动力学问题提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Addressing nonlinear numerical problems in quantum computing remains challenging, but several approaches, including Carleman linearization (CL), have been proposed to construct quantum algorithms for such problems. In this work, we develop a CL-based method for a high-dimensional nonlinear stochastic differential equation (SDE) $\mathrm{d} \mathbf{X}_t = \left(A(t) \mathbf{X}_t + F(t) \mathbf{X}_t^{\otimes s}\right) \mathrm{d} t + B(t) \mathrm{d} \mathbf{W}_t$ for an $\mathbb{R}^N$-valued random process $\mathbf{X}_t$, aiming at quantum speed-up with respect to the dimension $N$. We introduce auxiliary processes $\mathbf{Y}^j_t$ that approximate $\mathbf{X}_t^{\otimes j}$, derive a linearized system of SDEs for them, and obtain the linear ordinary differential equation (ODE) governing their expectations. We then apply a quantum ODE solver to it and, from the resulting quantum state, extract an estimate for the expectation of a linear function of $\mathbf{X}_t$ by overlap estimation. We show that, provided the nonlinear and noise terms in the SDE are sufficiently small relative to the linear term, the CL error decreases exponentially with the truncation order $J$ in CL, and we can estimate the target expectation with a ${\rm polylog}(N)$ number of queries to unitaries encoding coefficient matrices $A$, $F$, and $B$. Finally, we also run a numerical demonstration for an SDE related to Langevin dynamics, observing the exponential decay of the CL error as $J$ increases.

</details>

To the best of our knowledge, this is the first field demonstration of a fully integrated common-aperture architecture where a co-propagating classical optical link simultaneously performs full QKD post-processing and ecrypted payload transmission over a 10 km atmospheric channel. The classical link utilizes Manchester-coded on-off keying (MC-OOK) at 10 MHz, achieving a packet error rate of $\sim$ 0.4 $\%$ (estimated bit error rate (BER) of 3.13 $\times$ 10$^{-5}$) with hardware-based self-synchronization and error-packet retransmission. Over the 10.31 km link, the QKD system achieved an average quantum bit error rate (QBER) of 2.512 $\%$ and an average secret key rate of 644 bps using rate-adaptive low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes. Finally, using the distilled keys, real-time AES-256 encrypted image transmission was executed over the co-aligned classical optical channel, proving the feasibility of a completely self-contained, jam resistant mobile quantum optical network.

Abstract: Waveguides provide a flexible platform for long-range interactions between superconducting qubits. For a single-mode waveguide, we find that \(XY\) and \(ZZ\) interactions cannot be simultaneously suppressed to zero and the residual qubit-qubit coupling always exists. For example, the \(XY\) interaction may be suppressed to a negligible level when two qubits and the waveguide mode satisfy the two-photon resonant condition, but the \(ZZ\) interaction cannot be switched off. For multimode configurations within the central dispersive window, the engineered interaction types are very different for odd and even numbers of modes. Full-Hamiltonian simulations of minimal two- and three-mode configurations confirm \(XY\)-dominated SWAP dynamics and \(ZZ\)-dominated CZ-type dynamics, respectively. These results establish a frequency assignment criterion of qubits and waveguide modes for selectively engineering long-range qubit-qubit interaction types induced by the waveguide with finite multimode.

Result: New Structure IV攻击较Li等结果时间复杂度降低2^{n/2}（n为子密钥长度）；LBlock区分器比Liu等自动搜索区分器多1轮，使r轮密钥恢复复杂度降低2^{16}；15轮攻击子密钥猜测空间由76比特降至52比特，复杂度由O(2^{38})降至O(2^{26})，约降2^{12}。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of quantum computing, the security of traditional cryptographic systems is facing significant challenges. As a fundamental component of symmetric cryptography, block ciphers have attracted increasing attention regarding their quantum security in recent years.

First, we construct an 8-round quantum distinguisher for New Structure IV and perform an $r(r>12)$-round quantum key-recovery attack. Compared with Li et al.'s qCPA-based result presented at INSCRYPT 2024, our attack reduces the time complexity by a factor of $2^{n/2}$, where $n$ denotes the subkey length. Second, we design a 9-round quantum distinguisher for LBlock and extend it to an $r(r>15)$-round quantum key-recovery attack. The proposed quantum distinguisher covers one more round than Liu et al.'s automated-search distinguisher at ASIACRYPT 2025, which results in a $2^{16}$ reduction in the time complexity of our $r$-round quantum key-recovery attack on LBlock. Furthermore, by analyzing the structure of the round function and the key scheduling of LBlock, we reduce the subkey guess space for the 15-round attack from 76 bits to 52 bits, lowering the time complexity from \(O(2^{38})\) to \(O(2^{26})\) by a factor of \(2^{12}\).

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 把原始计数建模为逐光子二项损耗下的多项分布观测，通过贝叶斯后验联合推断理想输出分布和损耗率，自然处理碰撞输出，并提供可信区间。

Abstract: Photon loss is the dominant noise source in linear optical quantum circuits. Each lost photon turns an ideal n-photon outcome into a pattern with fewer photons, so the detector counts no longer follow the ideal output distribution, and the lossless events that do follow it become exponentially rare as the photon number grows. Discarding the lossy events (postselection) therefore wastes most of the data at realistic loss rates, and the ideal distribution has to be inferred from the lossy counts themselves. Recycling-based mitigation (Mills & Mezher, Quantum 2026; Quandela's photon_recycling) reweights lossy samples to recover lossless probabilities, but its estimators are biased and restricted to the no-collision sector, where each output mode holds at most one photon. We instead treat the raw counts as Multinomial observations under per-photon binomial loss and infer the ideal output distribution and the loss rate jointly from a Bayesian posterior, which extends naturally to outputs with collisions. On data from the independent Perceval simulator at the scale of prior work (n=4 photons in m=20 and m=10 modes), the posterior has lower KL divergence than both recycling and postselection at every tested loss rate in the no-collision benchmark. In the collision regime it recovers the loss rate and the collision-pattern probabilities, which recycling cannot estimate, and stays below recycling at every loss rate and below postselection except at low loss, where the two are comparable. Its advantage over recycling grows with the sample count, and it supplies credible intervals that the baselines lack.

</details>

Abstract: Coexistence of classical and quantum light in optical fiber is a key requirement for scalable quantum networks. However, it is fundamentally limited by nonlinear crosstalk from intense classical signals. Hollow-core fiber (HCF), with its intrinsically low optical nonlinearity, offers a promising solution. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the coexistence of polarization-entangled photons with dense wavelength-division multiplexed (DWDM) classical signals with a total launch power of 11.2 dBm, and perform a direct comparison between equal-length single-mode fiber (SMF) and photonic-bandgap-based HCF deployed in a campus network. Our results provide clear experimental evidence of the advantage of HCF and establish its potential as a transmission medium.

Abstract: We develop smooth hybrid lattice surgery for group surface codes, enabling joint logical measurements through merge and split operations along smooth boundaries. The code patches may correspond to identical or different finite groups, including non-Abelian groups. Using a tensor-network representation, we derive explicit measurement circuits and develop procedures for correcting nonidentity measurement outcomes. We illustrate the framework through a generalized controlled multiplication gate between group surface codes and protocols for preparing the $T$, $CZ$, $CCX$, and $CS$ magic states, producing resources encoded in one or more $\mathbb{Z}_2$ surface code patches. Our results show how smooth hybrid lattice surgery broadens the range of logical operations available for group surface codes.

Result: 轴对齐二分下，Néel序在Rényi-2系综中消失；垂直于纠缠边界的VBS分量在热力学外推中保持有限，且与平行分量不等价（各向异性）。45°倾斜二分下，两个VBS分量在数值误差内等价。二者共享同一体连续理论，因此差异只能归因于二分所实现的微观缺陷对称性不同。

Abstract: Universal information extracted from entanglement at a critical point is commonly associated with the long-distance properties of the bulk state, while microscopic details of a lattice bipartition are expected to be washed out under coarse graining. For a standard axis-aligned bipartition, the VBS component perpendicular to the entanglement boundary remains finite in the thermodynamic extrapolation and is inequivalent to the parallel component, while the Néel order vanishes. For a $45^\circ$ tilted bipartition, in contrast, the two VBS components remain equivalent within numerical accuracy. We interpret this contrast in terms of the Rényi twist defect: the two bipartitions have the same bulk theory but realize different microscopic defect symmetries. Our results demonstrate that lattice information invisible to the bulk continuum theory alone can remain active in the long-distance Rényi ensemble, providing a microscopic explanation for the strong bipartition dependence previously observed in entanglement studies of deconfined criticality.

Abstract: Stabilizer Rényi moments depend on a choice of operator basis and need not be determined by entanglement spectra. We derive an exact entanglement baseline for reductions of pure stabilizer states and a symmetry selection rule for the charged contribution at fixed support. An exact Ising example shows that an on-site rotation changes the full decay exponent while preserving every subsystem entanglement spectrum. For growing Ising intervals at index two, we obtain an exact Pfaffian representation and compare the charged amplitude with a candidate conformal boundary calculation. The data support this prediction, but its identification with the lattice limit still requires endpoint matching and control of the order of limits.

Abstract: We advance the compact dressed Hamiltonian (CDH) framework to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems strongly coupled to structured bosonic environments. The CDH approach combines reaction-coordinate mapping with a polaron transformation, incorporating strong system-environment hybridization into a dressed representation before truncating the collective bosonic mode. As a result of this truncation, dynamical features are captured with substantially fewer states than in the original representation. The residual environment is treated using a weak-coupling Markovian quantum master equation. We benchmark the method for spin-boson-type models including a single spin, coupled spins, and spin ensembles, considering both resonant and off-resonant regimes and weak to strong system-bath coupling. Comparisons with hierarchical equations of motion and converged reaction-coordinate calculations demonstrate that the CDH representation systematically approaches the reference dynamics with a compact Hilbert space, accurately reproducing oscillation frequencies, relaxation behavior, and long-time populations over a broad parameter regime. The method further captures cooperative phenomena, including bath-mediated interactions, synchronization between spins, and signatures for collective emission. Beyond efficient time propagation, the CDH framework provides direct access to the spectrum and eigenmodes of the Liouvillian. We demonstrate that slow dynamical modes converge rapidly in the CDH representation, enabling identification of relaxation timescales without fitting time-dependent observables. As a whole, the CDH framework provides an efficient and physical description of strongly coupled open quantum-system dynamics.

</details>

Abstract: How macroscopic definite outcomes and nonlocal correlations emerge from entangled superpositions across spacelike-separated regions without superluminal action or {\it ad hoc} wavefunction collapse remains a defining open question in the foundations of quantum mechanics. Here, we resolve this century-long paradox within \textit{linear} relativistic quantum electrodynamics by developing an open-system and measurement-defined framework realized in dual, spacelike-separated nonlinear crystals driven synchronously by a multi-mode optical frequency comb and seeded by an ultra-narrow coherent laser. Expanding the transverse electromagnetic vector potential into discrete single-photon frequency-comb supermodes, we show that tracing out the unmeasured continuous-wave idler field yields an exact spatial dephasing Lindblad master equation whose decoherence rate is governed by the non-orthogonal state overlap of single-photon-added coherent states. Connecting this open-system map to free-vector-space quotient measurement theory, we demonstrate that single-particle interference arises from the geometric projection of the composite state onto detector-defined conditional cosets rather than remote collapse, establishing strict operational equivalence across pump, seed, and signal phase scans. The resulting wave-particle-entanglement triality equality $P^2 + E^2 + V^2 = 1$ is saturated across the complete parameter space, while the atomic-clock-referenced comb structure anchors continuous-variable time-frequency displacement stabilizers directly at the hardware layer. Our work unifies induced coherence, Bohr's complementarity, and quotient measurement theory, providing a microscopic pathway toward fault-tolerant bosonic quantum information processing.

*Tai Hyun Yoon*

Empirical data demonstrate the superior convergence rate exhibited by the bidirectional algorithm over a unidirectional, forward-only sampling approach. This superiority is due to the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm in finding and weighting paths with non-zero amplitude in an extremely sparse path space. Sum of paths algorithms exhibit very favourable space complexity. Monte Carlo replaces exhaustive enumeration of the exponentially large path space by stochastic sampling, the computational cost being determined by the variance of the estimator. This work demonstrates these two features by presenting results on very large quantum circuits, with up to 4096 qubits.

In the one-photon model, the overlap between coherent Juddian branches vanishes in the thermodynamic or infinite frequency-ratio limits, asymptotically suppressing the FOT cusp. In the two-photon model, squeezed Juddian states lose normalizability at spectral collapse, thereby terminating the physical FOT line at finite $N$. Although the cusp strength tends to zero as the endpoint is approached, the terminating FOT retains a collective spectral signature: the different-parity gap is predicted to close with an $N$-dependent exponent $μ_N=(2N+3)/4$, one greater than the cusp termination exponent. By contrast, the same-parity soft mode retains $N$-independent square-root scaling and governs the critical response. Abstract: High-fidelity entangling gates are essential for quantum information processing. In transmon circuits, higher excited states provide additional pathways for engineering interactions within the computational subspace. Here we demonstrate controlled-Z (CZ) gates that exploit two noncomputational states, $\ket{02}$ and $\ket{20}$, through selective Floquet coupling. The nontrivial parametric modulation can activate both auxiliary transitions from $\ket{11}$ while suppressing the leading exchange between $\ket{01}$ and $\ket{10}$. Our results confirm that such fluctuations account for up to 93$\%$ of the error budget under the operating conditions of the gate implemented by Faorlin et $al.$. We identify target parameters to reduce the motional error from the reported $10^{-3}$ to the $10^{-5}$ level.

The framework is verified against analytical optical, virtual-state, and finite-key relations and evaluated against reported experimental COW-QKD operating points. The Kato finite-key calculation reproduces the refined experimentally reported secure-key rates most closely at intermediate distances, with deviations of -1.5% at 50 km and -10.3% at 75 km. Abstract: Cloud-accessible quantum computing has made heterogeneous Quantum Processing Units (QPUs) available through provider platforms that differ in qubit technology, calibration state, queueing behavior, and pricing model. Abstract: We give a bounded-error quantum polynomial-time algorithm for finding hidden parabolic subgroups of $\mathrm{GL}_n(R)$ over any effectively presented finite chain ring $R$. For chain length at least two, elementary abelian socle probes first recover the complete flag modulo the maximal ideal. When the residue field has size $q\ge3$, we reconstruct the flag recursively, lifting rank-one terminal members one digit at a time. The intermediate digits reduce to abelian hidden subgroup problems, while the final digit is a finite-field multiple-shift problem solved using learning from subset and Fourier sampling. Abstract: Early studies on the Dicke model linked superradiance to quantum chaos, whereas later anisotropic Dicke and Rabi studies reached apparently conflicting conclusions. However, the anisotropic Dicke model can be chaotic in the normal phase and regular in the superradiant phase, making superradiance neither necessary nor sufficient for global chaos. Abstract: Long-range interactions in many-body quantum systems give rise to a wide range of dynamical phenomena, from collective dynamics well described by integrable mean-field models with symmetric all-to-all couplings, to fast scrambling of quantum information from disordered long-range interactions. Here, we investigate scenarios in which both regimes emerge within a single deterministic, translation-invariant XY spin model with long-range couplings. They however travel along fabricated tracks rather than free space: permutations an atom array obtains by moving tweezers must here become collision-free motions on a physical lattice. We call it NeST, for Nested Shuttling Tracks. It yields trace and diamond mixing times of order $O(\log N+\log(1/ε))$. The static estimate also yields a system-size-independent CMLSI for the single-site Petz heat-bath and regularised heat-bath dynamics.

Abstract: The Gutzwiller projection produces a well-known ansatz for spin-liquid and quantum Hall ground-state wavefunctions that is amenable to numerical simulation, with the Laughlin wavefunction being the most celebrated example. Here, we clarify this issue by providing a field-theoretic description of the Gutzwiller projection. In a path integral description, the universal physics of the projected state is described in terms of a gauge field supported on a temporal slab of vanishing width $ε\rightarrow 0$ coupled to the bulk matter fields. We analytically derive the universal physics of various Gutzwiller projected states. Our construction is also applicable to discrete symmetries, and we use it to analyze various projected SPT states. Among our results, we show that the Gutzwiller projection is not a good ansatz for the long-distance properties of $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquids, spinon Fermi surface spin liquids or composite Fermi liquids, and that the Gutzwiller projection of a quantum spin Hall state has only quasi-long range order. In contrast, in examples where the gauge field is gapped, including Laughlin states, $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquids and Luttinger liquids, we show that the slab gauge theory from Gutzwiller projection reproduces the correct universal physics of the full parton gauge theory. We also present a generalization of our field theory that describes $G$-intertwining defects, with application to self-duality defects of recent theoretical interest.

</details>

Abstract: Hidden-order phases elude the conventional Ginzburg-Landau framework: although they spontaneously break symmetry, their order remains invisible to standard local two-point correlators. Instead, they are typically characterized by nonlocal (string) order parameters and are believed to underlie a range of exotic quantum states, such as symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases. While hidden correlations have previously been detected through nonlocal observables in quantum gas microscope experiments, in realistic solid-state systems multipoint observables are typically inaccessible and the signatures of hidden order remain largely unexplored. In this work, we show that hidden order in doped spin chains can be directly probed through dynamical two-point response functions measurable by resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) and neutron scattering. Focusing on a minimal model, we numerically identify clear spectral fingerprints of both the hidden-order phase, its associated hidden quantum critical point as well as a specific group of gapless SPT phases. Complementing our numerical results with analytical insights, we uncover the microscopic origin of these spectral signatures and demonstrate how they emerge from the interplay between spin and charge degrees of freedom. Together, our results establish spectroscopy as a practical probe of hidden order and gapless SPT phases and provide a unified understanding of the low-energy physics governing these unconventional quantum phases.

Abstract: Recent inelastic neutron scattering (INS) investigations of the kagome metal TbMn6Sn6 reveal novel chiral magnetic excitations associated with the kagome layer. Here, we use INS to show that chiral excitations are also found in RMn6Sn6 compounds with R=Tb, Ho, Er, and Y having uniaxial, tilted, planar ferrimagnet, and planar double-spiral magnetic structures, respectively. Additionally, R=Er and Y demonstrate that the chiral mode intensity is weaker in planar magnetic structures, suggesting that excitations are transverse to the local Mn moment direction. The chiral excitations appear only at L=odd positions in the Brilloun zone, and correspond to chiral spin exciations of the same handedness within each kagome layer, and pi out-of-phase between layers.

Abstract: Orbital magnetization is an elementary thermodynamic response function of a quantum system: the derivative of the grand potential with respect to magnetic field. Conclusion: GG粒子数可作为一种无需twist averaging即可有效抑制单粒子有限尺寸误差的系统尺寸选取策略，其DMC外推精度与TA相当，为周期体系QMC计算提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Single-particle finite-size errors remain a central obstacle in quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations of periodic systems, conventionally reduced via twist averaging (TA). Here we introduce Good-$Γ$ (GG) particle numbers as system sizes $N$ at which the $Γ$-point Hartree--Fock (HF) energy of the high-density two-dimensional uniform electron gas (2D UEG) lies anomalously close to its thermodynamic-limit (TL) value. All results in this work concern the 2D UEG in its high-density Fermi-liquid regime, $r_s=1$--$4$. We systematically investigate the kinetic- and exchange-energy errors, $ΔKE(N)$ and $ΔEX(N)$, that underlie this one-body finite-size-error cancellation across four densities and both spin polarizations. We then compare diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) energies extrapolated to the TL from GG-$N$ sequences against twist-averaged extrapolations at the same densities and polarizations, finding agreement to within a fraction of a millihartree in every case, independent of whether a $1/N$ or an $N^{-5/4}$ extrapolation form is used.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏离半满且高于Néel温度的三维Hubbard模型是含反铁磁自旋涨落的顺磁金属，但缺乏受控的动量分辨结果，且需为DMFT的扩展与自旋涨落理论提供基准。

Abstract: Away from half filling and above its Neel temperature, the three-dimensional Hubbard model is a paramagnetic metal with antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations, where controlled momentum-resolved results have been lacking. Yet the peak height is set by the slightly smaller value of this interaction at the peak wave vector, and near magnetic order this small difference changes the height by a large factor. Two-particle self-consistent theory reproduces the peak because its sum rule fixes its single constant interaction near this value, but for n = 0.7-0.9 at T/t = 0.14 its self-energy overestimates the local scattering rate and underestimates the momentum-dependent part. Though strong, the fluctuations are short ranged, with a correlation length of at most about two lattice spacings, and for n = 0.7-0.9 and T/t >= 0.14 the scattering rate near the Fermi surface varies by at most about 1% at fixed band energy, without hot spots. At U/t = 4 dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reproduces the local scattering rate and quasiparticle weight within a few percent, and the nonlocal part it misses is approximately a function of the band energy. Motivation: 神经网络分数量子霍尔态有望描述朗道能级混合，但现有工作对物理先验的引入仅停留在拟设内建的极小精确约束层面：先验过少（如MHPO）导致优化困难，先验(如通量附着)刚性施加则限制网络表达能力。

Method: 在球面几何下工作，以单极谐波乘积轨道(MHPO)为基础，用可学习的轨道对因子(orbital pair matrix factor)替换固定Laughlin结构，并令其同时依赖于电子构型与轨道，构成MHPO-M拟设；另采用因子化的轨道读出(readout)实现大体系扩展。

Result: 在ν=1/5、1/3、2/5下MHPO-M在所测变体中变分能量最低；基于其因子化轨道读出可扩展至35个电子，能量与三朗道能级精确对角化推断的趋势相当。

Conclusion: 物理先验可在不牺牲神经网络波函数灵活性的前提下恰当融入，为含朗道能级混合的FQH体系提供准确且可扩展的建模路径。

Abstract: Neural network fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states offer a promising alternative for capturing Landau level (LL) mixing and for understanding FQH physics, yet the incorporation of physical priors has so far been limited to the minimal exact constraints built into the ansatz. Working in spherical geometry, we show that the network's flexibility suffers both when only minimal additional physical priors are supplied and when priors such as flux attachment are imposed rigidly. In the former case, the monopole harmonic product orbital (MHPO) ansatz is difficult to optimize, whereas in the latter, the expressivity of the neural network is restricted. We therefore propose the orbital pair matrix factor MHPO (MHPO-M) ansatz, which balances physical priors against flexibility by not only replacing the fixed Laughlin structure with learnable pair factors but also making them dependent on both the electron configurations and the orbitals. Across $ν=1/5$, $1/3$, and $2/5$, MHPO-M achieves the lowest variational energy among the tested variants, and a factorized orbital readout based on it enables scaling to 35 electrons, with energies competitive with trends inferred from three-LL exact-diagonalization results. These results demonstrate that physical priors can be appropriately incorporated without sacrificing the flexibility of neural network wavefunctions, opening a promising path toward accurate, scalable modeling of FQH systems with LL mixing.

</details>


### [80] [Field theories and critical excitations at anyon condensation transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28148)
*Luisa Eck,Xie Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出两种互补方法，从母拓扑序与凝聚体的范畴数据中提取任意子凝聚相变的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子凝聚相变（尤其凝聚任意子为非阿贝尔时）难以刻画，缺乏仅依赖范畴数据的判据。

Method: 方法一：将针对1+1维相变、在SymTFT框架下发展的Ginzburg-Landau程序推广到2+1维任意子凝聚相变，适用于参与凝聚的任意子具有整数量子维数的情况。方法二：给出判定连续相变中哪些点激发与圈激发变为临界的准则：先确定哪些任意子圈始终保持凝聚，其余圈变为临界；点激发当且仅当其与所有凝聚圈的编织为平凡时变为临界。

Result: 在若干阿贝尔与非阿贝尔例子中验证了上述两种方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 范畴数据足以刻画任意子凝聚相变的临界激发谱，两种方法互为补充并可推广至非阿贝尔情形。

Abstract: Anyon condensation transitions remain challenging to characterize especially when the condensing anyons are non-abelian. We develop two complementary approaches for extracting information about the transition from the categorical data of the parent topological order and the condensate. The first adapts a Ginzburg-Landau procedure developed for $1+1$D transitions in the SymTFT setting to $2+1$D anyon condensation transitions. The construction applies when the anyons participating in the condensate have integer quantum dimensions. The second gives a criterion for identifying which point and loop excitations become critical at a continuous transition. The key step is to determine which anyon loops remain condensed throughout. All other loops become critical, while a point excitation becomes critical if it braids trivially with every condensed loop. We illustrate both proposals in several abelian and non-abelian examples.

</details>


### [81] [Proliferation Transitions for Quantum Doubles of Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28253)
*Yuhan Gai,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用对称性拓扑场论(SymTFT)框架系统研究量子双模型D(G,ω)中的增殖相变，证明相变性质可由可凝聚代数𝒜的分类数据直接读出，并揭示LG*型相变不仅依赖𝒜中的任意子场内容，还由𝒜的乘法结构决定立方相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增殖相变是2+1维拓扑序𝒯与其通过凝聚代数𝒜中任意子得到的𝒯/𝒜之间的相变。已有工作缺乏对这类相变（尤其是有ω三余循环的量子双模型）的统一、可计算刻画，且不清楚可凝聚代数中哪些数据真正决定相变性质与临界理论的具体形式。

Method: 以有限群G（可含三余循环ω）的量子双𝒯=D(G,ω)为对象，利用其可凝聚代数已被完全分类的优势；给定𝒜，将𝒯表示为针对该𝒜增殖相变定制的SymTFT，其体(bulk)由代数生成子范畴的Müger中心（透明线范畴，可含玻色子或费米子）构造；再由代数分类数据推断相变性质，并对gauged LG*型相变分析立方相互作用与𝒜乘法结构的关系。

Result: 建立了由Müger中心构造SymTFT体的方案，使相变性质可直接从代数分类数据读出；发现gauged LG*型相变不仅依赖𝒜中任意子的场内容，其立方相互作用还由𝒜的乘法结构固定；对具有相同任意子内容但乘法结构不同的孪生代数(twin algebras)，证明其诱导出彼此不同的增殖相变。

Conclusion: 可凝聚代数的乘法结构（而非仅任意子内容）是决定增殖相变与临界理论的关键数据；SymTFT/Müger中心的方法为量子双模型中所有增殖相变提供了统一且可计算的分类与推断框架，孪生代数为区分这些相变提供了明确判据。

Abstract: Proliferation transitions are phase transitions between a 2+1d topological order $\mathcal{T}$ and another $\mathcal{T}/\mathcal{A}$, obtained by condensing anyons in an algebra $\mathcal{A}\subset \mathcal{T}$. We consider such transitions in quantum doubles of groups, $\mathcal{T}=D(G, ω)$, for finite groups $G$ possibly with three-cocycle $ω$, for which condensable algebras are fully classified. Given a condensable algebra $\mathcal{A}$, we represent $\mathcal{T}$ in terms of a Symmetry Topological Field Theory (SymTFT), that is tailored to the $\mathcal{A}$ proliferation transition. The SymTFT bulk is constructed from the Müger center of the subcategory generated by the algebra, i.e. the category of transparent lines, which can be bosons or fermions. Properties of the transition can then be inferred directly from the algebra classification data. For gauged Landau-Ginzburg (LG*) type transitions, we show that they do not only depend on the anyons in $\mathcal{A}$ through their field content, but the cubic interactions are fixed in terms of the multiplication structure of $\mathcal{A}$. Applied to twin algebras, which have the same anyon content but different multiplication structures, we show that twins result in distinct proliferation transitions.

</details>


### [82] [Classical spin liquids in the kagome Ising model, and their link with magnetoelasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28298)
*T. Vignau Costa,J. Colbois,G. L. Rossini,F. Mila,D. C. Cabra,S. A. Grigera,R. A. Borzi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究kagome晶格上最近邻Ising模型与弹性晶格畸变的耦合，通过积分掉晶格自由度导出含至第三近邻的kagome J1-J2-J3 Ising有效自旋模型，并用张量网络方法与经典Monte Carlo重新刻画其零温相图，发现三个具有广延零温熵与代数衰减自旋关联的相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome Ising模型及其三十余年前研究的基态有待在现代数值方法下重新审视；同时磁弹性耦合如何生成有效阻挫相互作用并导致"自阻挫"现象尚缺乏系统的相图层面解释。

Method: 对耦合弹性畸变的kagome最近邻Ising模型积分掉晶格自由度，得到kagome J1-J2-J3 Ising有效模型；采用张量网络方法并结合经典Monte Carlo模拟，计算零温相图、基态熵与结构因子，并追踪磁弹性耦合在有效相图中生成的轨迹。

Result: 识别出三个具有广延零温熵和代数衰减自旋关联的相；这些相并非局限于精细调节点或相边界，而占据参数空间的广延区域；其准长程序表现为结构因子中的Bragg型峰，并与Ising及Heisenberg模型中的多种相建立联系。

Conclusion: kagome J1-J2-J3 Ising模型存在稳定的、具准长程序的广延无序相；磁弹性耦合轨迹在有效相图中的走向可解释铁磁相互作用下未阻挫磁弹性模型中出现自阻挫的机制。

Abstract: We investigate a nearest-neighbor Ising model on the kagome lattice coupled to elastic lattice distortions. By integrating out the lattice degrees of freedom, we derive an approximate spin-only description in terms of a kagome \(J_1\)-\(J_2\)-\(J_{3}\) Ising model with interactions extending to third nearest-neighbors. Using tensor network methods complemented by classical Monte Carlo simulations, we revisit its zero-temperature phase diagram and characterize the ground states studied more than thirty years ago. We discover three phases with extensive zero-temperature entropy and algebraically decaying spin correlations. Importantly, these states are not confined to fine-tuned points or frontiers between phases, but occupy extended areas of parameter space. Their quasi long-range order is reflected in Bragg-like peaks in the structure factors; we relate this, and other properties, to diverse phases found in Ising and Heisenberg models. Finally, we trace the trajectories generated by the magnetoelastic coupling through the effective-model phase diagram, explaining the emergence of self-frustration for the unfrustrated magnetoelastic model with ferromagnetic interactions.

</details>


### [83] [Sum-over-poles framework for quantum embedding to approach the real-frequency axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28472)
*Alessandro Carbone,Massimo Capone,Nicola Marzari,Tommaso Chiarotti*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于求和极点(SOP)表示的实频率量子嵌入框架，用于固体的自洽杂质/片段嵌入，避免解析延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子嵌入（如DMFT）多在虚频计算，需要病态的解析延拓才能得到实频谱；本文希望在实轴附近直接完成自洽求解。

Method: 将晶格划分为周期性重复的相互作用片段，推导片段的嵌入Dyson方程并自洽求解；利用嵌入势的紧凑极点展开在靠近实轴处迭代，并引入极点-留数(pole-and-residue)动力学达到自洽解。该方案将作者先前的算法求逆方法推广到相互作用哈密顿量，与DMFT（尤其扩展杂质问题时）密切相关。

Result: 在half filling的一维Hubbard环上，与DMFT的精确对角化实现进行基准对比验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为周期体系及材料中强关联缺陷的实空间动力学量子嵌入提供了自然途径。

Abstract: We introduce a real-frequency framework for the quantum embedding of solids, based on sum-over-poles (SOP) representations. We partition the lattice into periodically repeated interacting fragments and derive an embedded Dyson equation for the fragment, to be solved self-consistently. The resulting scheme generalizes the algorithmic-inversion method of our previous work to interacting Hamiltonians and is closely related to dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), especially when an extended impurity problem is introduced. By exploiting the compact pole expansion of the embedding potential, we carry out the self-consistent procedure close to the real axis, which avoids ill-posed analytic continuation. In addition, the SOP representation allows us to introduce a pole-and-residue dynamics, which we use to reach the self-consistent solution. After deriving the method, we benchmark it against exact-diagonalization implementations of DMFT on the one-dimensional Hubbard ring at half filling. The framework offers a natural route to real-space dynamical quantum embedding, for periodic systems as well as for strongly correlated defects in materials.

</details>


### [84] [Acceleration of CTQMC via Denoising Diffusion Models with Auxiliary Spectral Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28542)
*Rishi Rao,Li Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的神经去噪器，对CTQMC虚时格林函数去噪，可减少10–100倍测量步数并免费获得近似谱函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTQMC杂质求解器数值精确但昂贵，欠采样带来的统计噪声在低温研究中尤为严重，尤其在自能采样中会破坏Dyson方程自洽循环的稳定性。

Method: 用合成数据训练基于扩散模型架构的去噪网络，输出与含噪虚轴CTQMC数据一致的正定归一化辅助谱函数；再用自动微分按自能、其前两个高频矩及杂质占据数进行精修（类似CRM方法），从而在构造上保证格林函数与自能满足因果性。

Result: 测量步数减少10–100倍。半满Bethe晶格Hubbard模型仅用5000次测量即重现Mott转变；二维Hubbard模型DCA（两patch）用5×10^5次测量重现赝能隙转变；SrVO3谱函数在测量量减少50倍下与参考一致。

Conclusion: 该去噪框架可显著降低CTQMC测量成本，且因生成辅助谱函数而无需解析延拓即可获得系统近似谱函数。

Abstract: Continuous-time Quantum Monte Carlo (CTQMC) impurity solvers are a numerically exact, but expensive, option for solving the Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) equations, with statistical noise from undersampling presenting a major challenge to low temperature studies. This noise is especially egregious within the sampled self-energy, which can destabilize the self-consistency loop upon insertion into the Dyson equation. In this work, we introduce a neural denoiser for fermionic imaginary time Green's functions based on the diffusion model architecture trained on synthetic data. The network predicts positive and normalized auxiliary spectral functions consistent with the noisy imaginary axis CTQMC data, which is then refined via autodifferentiation to match the measured self-energy, its first two high-frequency moments, and impurity occupation similar to the Constrained Residual Minimization (CRM) method. This leads to Green's functions and self-energies that are causal by design. Through this denoising framework we are able to reduce the required CTQMC measurement steps by a factor of 10-100. We benchmark our method with a half-filled Hubbard model on a Bethe lattice, a two-patch Dynamical Cluster Approximation (DCA) of the two-dimensional Hubbard model, and the SrVO$_3$ system. We reproduce the Mott transition upon increasing Coulomb interaction for the half-filled Hubbard model with only 5000 CTQMC measurements, the pseudogap transition for the DCA two-dimensional Hubbard model with only 5$\times$10$^5$ measurements, and match the spectral function of SrVO$_3$ with 50 times fewer measurements than the reference. Since we generate auxiliary spectral functions, we recover the approximate spectral functions of the systems for free without performing analytic continuation.

</details>

### [85] [Exact spectral integrability of a finite-channel random Schrödinger operator: from fermionization of the Dyson--Riccati gas to Kontsevich integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27154)
*Yan V. Fyodorov,Pierre Le Doussal*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文精确求解任意有限通道数N的准一维β=2手性白噪声随机矩阵薛定谔算子的Riccati动力学，用单个Airy Hankel τ函数表达态密度与Lyapunov增长率，并统一得到Lifshitz尾与大N谱边软化（宽度N^{-4/5}，由Painlevé I三尖瓣解刻画）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果多局限于单通道N=1的Halperin–Thouless公式；多通道情形缺乏精确可解描述，且有限通道Lifshitz尾与大N谱边软化两类极限通常被分开处理，作者希望建立统一框架并推测其普适性。

Method: 精确求解准一维随机矩阵薛定谔算子的Dyson–Riccati本征气动力学：在任意样本长度L下给出精确自由费米子表示；L→∞时用单个Airy Hankel τ函数表达态密度与总Lyapunov增长率；再将τ函数写成外向围道Kontsevich矩阵Airy积分，做鞍点与双标度分析。

Result: 得到N=1 Halperin–Thouless公式的多通道推广；统一导出有限通道态密度Lifshitz尾与大N谱边行为；N≫1时尖锐的N=∞谱边在N^{-4/5}标度窗口内软化，其剖面由Painlevé I的tritronquée解支配。

Conclusion: Riccati动力学与Airy Hankel τ函数给出该类β=2准一维模型的可积精确描述；作者推测该准一维标度谱边剖面对广泛一类β=2随机带矩阵具有普适性。

Abstract: We solve exactly, for arbitrary finite channel number $N$, the Riccati dynamics of a quasi-one-dimensional random matrix Schrödinger operator with Hermitian white-noise potential in the $β=2$ unitary symmetry class. The associated Dyson--Riccati eigenvalue gas admits an exact free-fermion representation at any sample length $L$, which in the limit $L\to \infty$ expresses the density of states and the total Lyapunov growth rate exactly through a single Airy Hankel $τ$-function providing multichannel generalization of $N=1$ Halperin--Thouless formulas. The $τ-$function further admits an outgoing-contour Kontsevich matrix Airy integral representation. Its saddle-point and double-scaling analyses give, in a unified way, the finite-channel density Lifshitz tail, and the large-$N$ spectral edge. In particular, for $N\gg 1$ the sharp $N=\infty$ edge is softened within an $N^{-4/5}$ spectral window, with its profile governed by the tritronquée solution of Painlevé I. We conjecture universality of this quasi-one-dimensional scaling edge profile for a broad class of $β=2$ random band matrices.

</details>


### [86] [Moments of Random Permanents and Hafnians: A Replica Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27872)
*Paolo Facchi,Angelo Mariano,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Antonello Scardicchio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 用永久量/Hafnian的围道积分表示将随机矩阵矩的计算转化为类平均场自旋玻璃的复本计算，得到复本对称鞍点下的闭式渐近结果，并在k=2处发现复本对称破缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵的永久量与Hafnian的矩（即高阶矩）此前缺乏精确的渐近刻画，而这类量在玻色采样等量子计算问题的复杂度分析中处于核心地位，需要一个可控的解析框架。

Method: 利用permanent与Hafnian的围道积分表示，把N×N独立元素随机矩阵的2k阶矩写成复本（replica）积分；对k>2取复本对称鞍点做鞍点近似，并在1/N阶做展开修正。

Result: 对k>2的2k阶矩，主鞍点为复本对称，给出指数增长率、前因子及首个1/N修正的闭式表达，且与精确枚举一致；增长率仅依赖元素为实或复，更高阶累积量只影响前因子与1/N项。k=2时复本对称解失稳，分布分裂为幂律主体与大偏差尾部，并给出尾部计算。

Conclusion: 该复本方法为随机矩阵永久量/Hafnian的矩提供了统一、可精确对照的渐近理论，并直接应用于单光子与压缩光玻色采样。

Abstract: Using contour-integral representations of the permanent and of the Hafnian, we turn the computation of their moments for random matrices into a replica calculation, analogous to that of the moments of the partition function of a mean-field spin glass. For the $2k$-th moment of $N\times N$ matrices with independent entries, the dominant saddle point is replica symmetric for $k>2$, and we obtain in closed form the exponential growth rate, the prefactor and the first $1/N$ correction, in agreement with exact enumerations. The growth rate depends only on whether the entries are real or complex; higher cumulants affect only the prefactor and the $1/N$ term. At $k=2$ the replica-symmetric solution becomes unstable, separating a power-law bulk of the distribution from a large-deviation tail, which we compute. The results apply to boson sampling with single photons and with squeezed light.

</details>


### [87] [Ubiquitous Flat Bands in Edge-Inflated Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28461)
*Richard Berkovits*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出"边膨胀"（edge-inflation）机制，证明平带并非周期晶格所独有，而可在多种非周期随机网络中普适出现：将种子图的每条边替换为一维紧束缚链或无环motif后，系统涌现大量简并平带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带传统上与周期晶格的几何阻挫或手征对称约束相关，被认为需要晶体序。作者希望检验平带是否可以在无序、非周期网络中普遍存在，并给出构造性机制与鲁棒性判据。

Method: 1) 以随机正则图、Voronoi镶嵌、Delaunay三角化、无标度网络、小世界网络和Erdős–Rényi图为种子图；2) 将每条边替换为定长L或平均长度L̄的随机长度一维紧束缚链，或更一般地替换为相同无环motif；3) 利用原种子顶点处的相消干涉构造平带本征态；4) 通过图零度η(G)与最大匹配数ν(G)分析零能平带的简并度；5) 数值扫描多种随机图系综，并施加种子位点无序、随机磁通、键无序与链位点无序以测试鲁棒性。

Result: 原始随机图邻接矩阵通常满秩、零本征态极少，而膨胀网络呈现广延平带；平带能量由链或相同无环motif的本征值决定；零能平带简并度与η(G)、ν(G)相关，数值上支持η(G)=N−2ν(G)（存在小幅偏差）；简并零能态比例主要由种子图平均度控制；种子位点无序与随机磁通几乎不改变平带，而键无序和链/motif位点无序使其展宽。

Conclusion: 平带不需要晶体序，可作为局部图膨胀与motif替换的稳健结果出现；该机制为在无序、非周期材料或网络平台中设计平带提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Flat bands are usually associated with periodic lattice geometries, where destructive interference or chiral-symmetry constraints produce macroscopically degenerate eigenstates. Here we show that flat bands arise generically in a broad class of nonperiodic random networks through a simple edge-inflation procedure. Starting from random regular, Voronoi tessellation, Delaunay triangulation, scale-free, small-world, and Erdös-Rényi seed graphs, each edge is replaced by a finite one-dimensional tight-binding chain, either with fixed length $L$ or with random lengths of mean $\bar L$. Although the original random graphs typically have adjacency matrices of full rank and therefore few or no zero eigenstates, their inflated counterparts exhibit extensive flat bands. The flat-band energies are determined by the eigenstates residing on the chains or, more generally, by the eigenvalues of identical loopless motifs replacing the seed bonds. The corresponding eigenstates are states supported on subsets of the inflated chains formed by destructive interference at the original seed vertices. For zero-energy flat bands, we relate the degeneracy to the graph nullity $η(G)$ and the maximum matching number $ν(G)$. The numerical results support the correspondence $η(G)=N-2ν(G)$, up to small deviations, across the random graph ensembles studied. The fraction of degenerate zero-energy states in inflated random graphs is controlled primarily by the average degree of the seed graph. We further show that the flat bands exhibit characteristic robustness to disorder: disorder on the seed sites and random magnetic flux leave the flat bands essentially unchanged, while bond disorder and disorder on the chain or motif sites broaden them. These results show that flat bands do not require crystalline order, but can emerge as robust consequences of local graph inflation and motif substitution.

</details>

### [88] [Steering of topological criticality in the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain via non-Hermitian substitutional impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27436)
*Juan A. Cañas,Guo-Qing Zhang,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在一维SSH链中引入有限浓度的非厄米替位杂质，并通过格点依赖的非互易胞内/胞间跃迁振幅实现拓扑相边界的确定性重构；杂质内部结构不对称可诱导临界点的宏观位移，从而以局部缺陷不对称性控制拓扑相的扩张或压缩，并伴随由非厄米趋肤效应几何表征的实-复谱转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种确定性机制，用以主动重构拓扑相边界，而非依赖随机无序的统计效应；并厘清孤立非厄米不对称性如何协同作用以移动宏观拓扑保护边界。

Method: 在SSH链中掺入有限浓度非厄米替位杂质，赋予其格点依赖的非互易胞内与胞间跃迁振幅；打破杂质跃迁的内部结构对称性以定向移动临界点；通过有限尺寸标度分析提取关联长度临界指数，并借助非厄米趋肤效应对谱转变作几何刻画。

Result: 临界点发生定向宏观位移，拓扑相可通过局部缺陷不对称性实现可控扩张或压缩；对称与不对称构型下的临界指数分别约为ν≈1.455与ν≈1.397，表明无序诱导偏离原始SSH普适类，且在高缺陷密度下指数发散；同时观察到杂质诱导的实谱到复谱转变，并由非厄米趋肤效应几何表征。

Conclusion: 孤立的非厄米不对称性可协同作用，确定性地移动宏观拓扑保护边界，为拓扑相边界的主动调控提供了可设计途径。

Abstract: We investigate the topological phase in a one-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain in the presence of a finite concentration of non-Hermitian substitutional impurities. Our primary focus is the discovery of a deterministic mechanism to actively reconfigure the topological phase boundaries by introducing site-dependent, non-reciprocal intracell and intercell hopping amplitudes. Specifically, breaking the internal structural symmetry of the non-reciprocal impurity hoppings induces a targeted macroscopic displacement of the critical point. This mechanism allows for the controlled expansion or compression of the topological phase strictly via local defect asymmetry. We validate this boundary displacement through finite-size scaling analysis. The extracted correlation length critical exponents ($ν\approx 1.455$ and $ν\approx 1.397$ for symmetric and asymmetric configurations, respectively) confirm a disorder-induced departure from the pristine SSH universality class, with the exponent diverging at high defect densities. We also report an impurity-induced real-to-complex spectral transition geometrically characterized by the Non-Hermitian Skin Effect. Ultimately, our results establish how isolated non-Hermitian asymmetries cooperatively interact to deterministically shift macroscopic topological protection.

</details>


### [89] [Zeeman Quantum Geometry of the Layer Pseudo-spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26982)
*Bingxuan Jiang,Shihao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文引入层赝自旋（layer pseudo-spin）的量子几何——由带间Berry联络与层矩阵元构成的混合张量，用以刻画位置依赖层间隧穿耦合的双层体系对振荡面内电场的线性与高阶响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维层间由位置依赖隧穿耦合的体系具有可调控的层赝自旋极化这一内禀自由度，但缺乏描述其对外场几何响应的理论框架；且层赝自旋为Ising型标量（三重旋转下不变），其选择定则不同于赝矢量自旋，需专门建立对应的量子几何量。

Method: 构造层赝自旋量子几何：基于带间Berry联络与层矩阵元的混合张量；推导三重旋转下的选择定则；在最小两波段模型中计算层赝自旋量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率及其布里渊区积分；对扭角MoTe₂双层定量计算局域偶极调制，并分析整流、螺旋性奇（helicity-odd）与三次谐波通道。

Result: 最小两波段中层赝自旋量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率非零而布里渊区积分消失，故面内驱动在linear order不改变空间平均极化，但写入moiré周期性的胞内图样，可由层分辨隧穿直接成像；在扭角MoTe₂双层中量化了局域偶极调制，并识别出净响应出现于更高阶所经由的整流、螺旋性奇与三次谐波通道。

Conclusion: 层赝自旋量子几何为层分辨光-物质耦合提供统一描述：线性阶仅产生可成像的moiré周期胞内极化图样，净极化响应须通过高阶通道（整流、螺旋性奇、三次谐波）实现，为扭角双层中的层赝自旋调控与探测提供理论依据。

Abstract: Two-dimensional layers coupled by position-dependent interlayer tunnelling host a layer pseudo-spin whose polarization is a controllable internal degree of freedom. We introduce the quantum geometry of this layer pseudo-spin: a mixed tensor formed from the interband Berry connection and the layer matrix element, which governs the response to an oscillating in-plane electric field. Because the layer pseudo-spin is an Ising-type scalar invariant under threefold rotation, its selection rules differ from those of a pseudovector spin. In a minimal two-band model, the layer-pseudo-spin quantum metric and Zeeman--Berry curvature are nonzero, while their zone integrals vanish. An in-plane drive therefore leaves the spatially averaged polarization unchanged at linear order but imprints a moiré-periodic intracell pattern, directly imageable by layer-resolved tunnelling. For twisted MoTe$_2$ bilayer, we quantify the local dipole modulation and identify the rectified, helicity-odd, and third-harmonic channels through which a net response appears at higher order.

</details>

Method: 扫描隧道显微镜/隧道谱（STM/STS）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、密度泛函理论（DFT）以及准粒子干涉（QPI）测量相结合。

Conclusion: NbIrTe4构成一个可通过局域结构操控有效调控表面能带色散的平台，为堆垛/位移工程调控关联与拓扑表面态提供依据。

Abstract: Ternary layered transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) offer high tunability and access to correlated and topological states. However, like the broader family of layered TMDCs, their properties are sensitive to stacking order and interlayer coupling. Here, we investigate the electronic structure and surface states of NbIrTe$_4$, a candidate Weyl semimetal in bulk, and predicted to be a quantum spin Hall insulator in monolayer form. Using scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM), angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), and density-functional theory (DFT), we attribute the strong resonance in tunnelling spectroscopy located approximately 8 meV below the Fermi level to a surface-derived flat band which shows sensitivity to the local stacking order at the surface. In regions where the top layer is laterally shifted, tunnelling spectroscopy instead shows a gap-like feature with intensity near the Fermi level shifted in energy. Combining DFT and quasiparticle interference (QPI) measurements, we find this dramatic change in the spectroscopy in the vicinity of the quasi-1D flat-band surface state becomes dispersive due to a change in the spatial distribution of the density of states in the top two layers. These findings establish NbIrTe$_4$ as a platform where surface band dispersion can be effectively tuned through local structural manipulation.

</details>

TL;DR: 菱面体石墨烯在向超导转变时出现量子振荡频率突增与多频结构；本文提出内空穴口袋的两种竞争归宿——坍缩为单连通费米液体或晶化为"反晶体"，可解释该频率跳变与多音频谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期菱面体石墨烯的量子振荡测量显示：随着载流子密度降低至超导区，主频突然增大并出现额外频率。非相互作用能带模型虽能给出多频，却无法解释频率的向上跳变，亟需一种超出能带图像的新机制。

Method: 围绕环形费米海中内空穴口袋的两种竞争命运展开分析：一为坍缩成单连通（简单连通）费米液体，二为晶化为"反晶体"（局域化空穴与巡游电子共存）。比较两者对内、外费米轮廓及磁击穿轨道的影响。

Result: 口袋坍缩在固定密度下使外部费米轮廓收缩；而空穴晶化则保留更大的费米轮廓，并支持磁击穿轨道。这一对比为观测到的频率跳变提供了可能解释；此外重构出的轨道产生多音（多频）振荡谱。

Conclusion: "反晶体"是菱面体石墨烯超导附近正常态的有力候选，量子振荡的频率跳变与多频结构可作为其鉴别性证据。

Abstract: Recent quantum-oscillation measurements in rhombohedral graphene reveal an abrupt increase in the dominant frequency and the emergence of additional frequencies as the carrier density is lowered toward superconductivity. Noninteracting band models can account for multiple frequencies but do not explain the upward jump. We show that these features can arise from two competing fates of the inner hole pocket in an annular Fermi sea: collapse into a simply connected Fermi liquid or crystallization into an anticrystal, in which localized holes coexist with itinerant electrons. Pocket collapse contracts the outer Fermi contour at fixed density, whereas hole crystallization preserves a larger contour that supports magnetic-breakdown orbits. This contrast provides a possible explanation for the frequency jump, while additional reconstructed orbits produce a multitone spectrum. Our results support the anticrystal as a candidate normal state near superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体具有动量空间中自旋劈裂的能带，可作为无外场自旋输运平台；然而其介观光干涉（量子环）中自旋分辨的干涉节点、通量周期性与晶体取向控制的自旋极化尚未被系统刻画，尤其缺少对有限宽度、多模、无序与退相干条件下极化鲁棒性的评估。

Result: 通量响应严格h/e周期；对称单模几何存在精确半通量透射零点；垂直场等效面积A_eff=1.0342πr₀²；交错磁劈裂把公共零点分裂为近邻自旋相关节点；晶体旋转使透射自旋反转，θ_A=π/4时精确自旋简并；50 nm环在亚特斯拉场下出现|P_T|>0.99窗口，最易实现者仅需均匀场；有限偏压、温度、无序、Zeeman及电压探针下极化稳定性显著优于单个相干共振幅度。

Conclusion: 干涉节点位置、h/e通量周期性与晶体取向可控的自旋极化构成交错磁介观干涉仪的实验可辨识特征，可为无外场或低场自旋干涉器件提供判据。

Abstract: We study coherent two-terminal transport through a finite-width $d$-wave altermagnetic quantum ring with nonmagnetic leads. A confined Aharonov--Bohm flux remains exactly $h/e$ periodic and, in the symmetric one-mode geometry, produces an exact half-flux transmission zero. A perpendicular field shifts the interference pattern according to an effective area $A_{\rm eff}=1.0342\,πr_0^2$ and the altermagnetic splitting resolves the common zero into nearby spin-dependent nodes. Rotating the crystal reverses the transmitted spin, whereas the mirror orientation $θ_A=π/4$ is exactly spin degenerate. For a $50$~nm reference ring, sub-tesla fields yield windows with $|P_T|>0.99$, the most accessible of which requires a uniform field alone and no confined flux. Finite-bias, thermal, disorder, Zeeman, and spin-conserving voltage-probe calculations show that the polarization is appreciably more stable than individual coherent-resonance amplitudes. These results identify interference-node positions, flux periodicity, and crystal-controlled spin polarization as experimentally useful signatures of an altermagnetic mesoscopic interferometer.

</details>

### [94] [Magnetic-Field-Induced Darkening of Bright Excitons in a MoWSe$_2$ Monolayer Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27469)

Method: 对合金半导体Mo0.68W0.32Se2在4.2 K下施加面内磁场（至25 T），测量亮/暗激子的能量与强度演化，并用亮-暗混合（级排斥）模型定量拟合；结合合金化使体系接近亮-暗交叉点（小亮-暗劈裂+强面内磁耦合）的机制分析。

Result: 25 T内亮激子发光被抑制约85%，并激活低14.2±1.3 meV的暗激子线；能量与强度随磁场的耦合演化可被混合模型定量描述，有效面内g因子|g∥|=5.2±0.2。
